# Gods of the Arena

Polyworld's 5v5 lane battler, and one of three BASIC file-player games
that went live on 2026-09-09. Two gods sit in opposite-corner forts with
three lanes, a river, a bridge and a pine jungle between them. Ten heroes,
footmen and eighteen towers fight for twenty minutes.

- Coworld: `cow_e8fbfdfd-8cce-4cd0-a046-796e3c287fb8` (`2026.9.9.3`)
- League: `league_3c60897b-25cf-4b37-9d1a-8554c1198f28` (Competition,
  30-minute rounds, `team_n` with two teams of five, distinct teammates,
  filler policies complete short rosters)
- Source: `~/p/polyworld/examples/gods_of_the_arena` at commit `028cc5f`
- Ours: **Khors**, `khors/khors.bas`

## Runtime

`player_runtime` is `game-hosted`. There is no container and no socket -
the artifact is one `.bas` file, uploaded with
`coworld upload-policy --file`. Full host surface in
[docs/player.md](docs/player.md).

## Upload

There is nothing to build. The artifact is the `.bas` file itself, and it
goes up with `--file`. The bundled `uv --project ~/p/metta run coworld`
predates the game-hosted file-player workflow and has no such flag, so
run the CLI out of a metta `origin/main` worktree:

    git -C ~/p/metta worktree add /tmp/metta-main origin/main
    CW="uvx --from /tmp/metta-main/packages/coworld[auth] coworld"
    $CW upload-policy --file games/gods-of-the-arena/khors/khors.bas --name khors
    $CW submit khors:vN -l league_3c60897b-25cf-4b37-9d1a-8554c1198f28

File policies take no `--run`, no `--secret-env` and no Bedrock flags.
**This league auto-champions**: `submit` reports `Auto champion: always`
without the flag being passed, so a submission that qualifies becomes the
champion. Submit only after hosted XP says the candidate is better.

## Scoring

Every hero on the team that destroys the enemy fort scores 1. **A match
that reaches the tick limit scores 0 for all ten heroes.** Standings are
platform Elo over those binary scores.

That single rule shapes everything: a hero that farms beautifully for
twenty minutes and never opens a lane has scored exactly as well as one
that stood still.

## Baseline

The bundled `players/base.bas` attacks the nearest living enemy of any
kind, drinks a potion under half health, buys a mana potion when dry,
buys a poison potion whenever an enemy is in sight, and works a per-class
equipment ladder. It never walks anywhere except 64, 64 when the screen
is empty.

Because "nearest enemy" happens to include exposed towers and forts, the
baseline does siege - but only by accident, and it will happily turn away
from a fort it could end the match on to hit a footman two tiles closer.

## Khors

The baseline plus exactly two changes, both of which measured well
locally and neither of which needed new state:

1. **The exposed fort outranks everything.** A fort is listed as alive
   only once one of its three lanes is cleared, so the tick it appears is
   the tick the match can be won. It has 400 health.
2. **No poison potions.** 40 gold for 35 damage, re-bought every time an
   enemy comes into sight, is the worst gold in the shop and it starves
   the equipment ladder.

## Local A/B, 2026-09-09

Headless binary built from the example (`nim c -d:headless -d:release`),
our bot on all five seats of one side against `base.bas` on the other,
counted on both sides because Red is structurally favoured. Wins for the
variant out of 24 games (12 seeds each side), first seed block:

| variant | change | wins |
|---|---|---|
| control | `base.bas` mirror | 10 |
| v1 | fort priority | 12 |
| v2 | fort, then tower, priority | 10 |
| **v3** | **fort priority, no poison** | **15** |
| v5 | v3 plus a class-free value ladder | 12 |
| v6 | v3 plus keeping three elixirs stocked | 10 |

Second, disjoint seed block of 16 each side: control 15/32, v3 18/32.
`khors.bas` is v3, verified by identical world hashes on two seeds.

Note that three plausible ideas all measured *worse* than doing nothing:
always preferring towers (dives without an escort), buying by raw stat
value (the class ladder's cheap early items matter more), and stocking
potions (gold spent on sustain is gold not spent on damage).

Local runs are a design tool only. Hosted XP requests decide.

## Ideas backlog

1. **Lane assignment.** `HeroLanes` is `[0, 0, 1, 2, 2]` by hero index and
   `selfId` is `100 + index`, so a script can work out its own lane and
   the tower it is supposed to be breaking, instead of the nearest one.
2. **Escort-aware diving.** Towers acquire footmen before heroes. Counting
   friendly footmen inside the tower's radius before committing should let
   a hero siege far more aggressively than "nearest enemy" allows - the
   naive version of this (v2) lost, but it had no escort condition at all.
3. **Execute range.** Enemy hero health is readable but max health is not,
   so an execute threshold has to be absolute. 220 was a guess; sweep it.
4. **Death is a full heal.** Nothing on the map regenerates health, and
   respawn costs 8 seconds and hands the killer 100 gold and 150 XP. There
   is a real question about whether a potion-less hero at 15 percent
   should walk home, keep farming, or throw itself at a tower.
5. **Late-game all-in.** The tick limit scores zero for everyone, so from
   perhaps tick 24000 onward every hero should ignore farming entirely and
   push the weakest lane, win or lose.
6. **Six elixirs, no gear.** Consumables stack to 8 at 90 health each. A
   single hero built entirely on sustain that never dies and never leaves
   a tower might out-siege a hero built on damage. v6 tested a weak
   version of this and lost, but it kept the full gear ladder as well.
 7. **Team chat codebook GC1** (user, 2026-10-09; James Botts' proposal:
   https://softmax.com/gods-of-the-arena/forum/post_d9317fa8-649b-4133-bafa-04df67190dfb
   and https://softmax.com/gods-of-the-arena/wiki/team-chat-codebook). League
   teams are mixed, so chat is the only way to coordinate. GC1 lines are
   `GC1 <seat> <hero> <tick> <VERB> [args]` (at most 96 bytes, 9 tokens) on
   the team channel: `sendChat(-1, text$)`, `pullMailbox$()`, `mailboxId()`.
   Verbs: HI/NEED (role, lane plan at the draft), LANE/FREE (claims with
   a fixed tie-break), PUSH/HOLD/FARM/BACK/ESC/GRP, FOCUS <id>,
   SIEGE <id> <x> <y>, HELP <x> <y> <hp>, SEEN, MISS, ST (status), ACK/NO.
   Chat is not in replays; only James Botts sends it so far. For our
   league Warlock: answer HELP and SIEGE calls (walk to the caller, ACK),
   announce `HI S R` and `ESC <seat>` for the carry it follows, and send
   SIEGE when our carry hits a guard. Parse defensively (bounded pulls,
   length and digit checks, team channel only): a runtime error disables
   the hero. Fast XP returns our bot's logs, so log the inbox to see who
   talks.
 8. **Hide behind heroes** (user, 2026-10-09). macromackie seems to keep
   allies between itself and the enemy so they take the damage, like a
   carry should. Measured with new gota_stats fields exposed_share,
   hide_hero and hide_creep (an allied hero or lane creep on the line to
   the nearest enemy hero whose attack range we are in); candidates v329
   (Warlock behind its carry) and v330 (Crossbowman screened).

## 2026-09-21: new rules week (engine 2026.9.21.3, cow_27feb885)

Drafting, ability points, buyback, portal scrolls, keep-only shopping,
ranged creeps, shared lane XP, towers 1900/2600/3900 HP (doubled), guards
3900 x2, god 400. Every pre-change bot is broken: old khors lost 0/4
locally (no draft, no spells, no shop). Hosted battles are 20 min after
the draft. Most base-vs-base games now time out (zero for all ten): base
mirror 5 decided of 20 at 20 min. Closing the game is the whole problem.

Timeline extractor (polyworld/tmp/gota_timeline/timeline.nim, built with
--path:examples/gods_of_the_arena -d:replayEvents): in the decided base
mirror (seed 1074) the loser died 45 times to the winner's 34, most by 2 min
(buyback loops back into the fight), and the winner's carries reached L12
vs L10. Every hero death pays the killer 100 gold and 150 XP; creeps pay 15
XP shared. Deaths are the economy. Next single changes: no buyback (e6),
buyback only the first death (e7), focus the weakest enemy hero in 8 tiles
(e10); each vs base over 24 games, with a v2-vs-base reference.

### 2026-09-21 16:05: new base.bas (777 lines) landed; seat effect; drafting

- New base (scored targeting, kiting, dodging, retreat, portals, explicit
  casting) vs khors v2: blue won 16/16 whichever bot sat there. v2 vs the
  old base: blue 23/24. But a regroup-vs-regroup mirror: RED 16/16. The
  seat effect depends on the bot, so it is an interaction with where the
  bots walk, not a map bias (structures are 180-degree symmetric; tower
  deaths in seed 1037 were red diving blue's mid/lane-2 towers).
- Local single changes vs the old base, 24 games (design signal only):
  v2 reference 13/24 (1 as red!), no buyback 14, buyback first death only
  16, focus weakest hero 17 (9/12 as red), tower respect 16, regroup 18
  (12/12 as red, 6/12 as blue).
- First pick over 32 seeds: see below. Per-class first pick vs new base,
  6 seeds a side: mostly undecided; Crossbowman first 6/12 (5 as blue);
  Lich first and Berserker first lost 5 as red; Warlock first lost 5 as
  blue. Too few decided games to rank heroes; need fixed-class matches.

### 2026-09-21 ~16:40: scoring changed (cow_e5445477, 2026.9.21.5)

Per-hero score = lifetime XP - 200 per simulated minute (draft included),
floored at 0, team-averaged; ladder = mean round score (rated_k 0.15),
random 24-game rounds. Victory only stops the clock. Local team-mean
(unfloored) scores at 20 min: new base mirror -756/-921, v2 vs new base
-176 (red) / +16 (blue), regroup vs new base -528 / -212. Nobody averages
200 XP/min, so after the floor almost every hero scores 0 and the mean is
carried by the few heroes that out-earn the clock. Only the last hitter
gets hero XP (150) and tower XP (100); creep XP (15/creep) is split among
heroes within 6 tiles with 15% to the last hitter. Hypothesis: funnel all
XP into one carry (others stay >6 tiles from creep deaths, feed last hits,
keep it alive), and never end a game the carry is out-earning.

| khors:v3 | funnel: one carry (class preference Crossbowman > Ranger > Berserker > Arcanist > Lich > DH > DK > VK > Warlock > Druid, ties by lower id) plays the new base; the other four shadow it 6 tiles toward home (outside the 6-tile creep XP radius), attack only enemy heroes near themselves or the carry, keep the base's retreat/buyback/shop | local floored score 289/74 vs base 277/161 (design only) | xreq_4eb82422 (red) team mean 460/game, xreq_4744a147 (blue) 571/game, rivals Games Bond v6 / Scott Smith gota-g003 v2 / daveey-2 codex v1 all 0 in all 24 games; carry scored 1,157-4,334 per game | NOT KEPT: v2 on the scored coworld (xreq_27945712 red, xreq_4dd6f562 blue) averages 566/game (629 red, 503 blue; several heroes per game clear the clock because games end early), v3 508; Welch t -0.89 |
| khors:v4 | v3, carry never targets the enemy god (a carry above 200 XP/min loses score when the game ends) | | xreq_22fafbe4 (red), xreq_6510cf0c (blue) vs the same top 3 | REVERT: 401 vs v3 508 (t -1.65); longer games cost more than the carry earns |
| khors:v5 | v3 with two carries (two best preference ranks), escorts shadow the nearer one | | xreq_61df0ea7 (red), xreq_ac6070bb (blue) | REVERT: 0 in all 24 games (all five heroes scored 0; two-carry selection is broken) |
| khors:v6 | v2 + regroup (walk to the living allies' centroid when >12 tiles away and nothing to fight); locally the only change that closed games (11/12) | | xreq_58540817 (red), xreq_eb06e758 (blue) | REVERT: 525 vs v2 566 (t -0.73) |
| khors:v7 | new base.bas verbatim, as a reference on the scored coworld | | xreq_a11095cf (red), xreq_3e80b9eb (blue) | REVERT: 244 vs 566 (t -5.71); the new base loses games it should win against these rivals |

Lesson: with score = XP - 200/min, a team that wins by minute 8-10 keeps
most of its heroes above zero (v2: 591/1086/805/343/573 in one game),
while a 20-minute farm leaves one carry positive. Short wins beat funnels.

### Field table, rounds 630-632 (mean reward per appearance): aaron-gota-micro0922-coach 1,653 (n 27), aaron-gota-micro0922 1,452 (27), richard 1,183 (40), relh 1,176 (40), Jordan 877 (40), khors v47 739 (39), arena-codex-draft 737, aaron-compat 380, base.bas 119, everything else 0-70.
aaron-gota-micro0922 (both names, one file, policy store hash b82c3799…, scratch aaron-gota-micro0922_v1.bas, 597 lines, machine-generated "gota-semantic-policy"): thinks every 6 ticks; draft Ranger 180 > Arcanist 170 > Crossbowman 160 > Lich 150 > Druid 140 > DK 130 > Warlock 120 with -25 per same-role teammate; lane = (own-team ordinal by player id) mod 3; ADVANCE = attack-move to the lane waypoint (corner or centre), and once within 8 tiles of it, attack-move at the ENEMY GOD for the rest of the game; targets creeps +200 (+650 for a last hit in reach, +25 ranged), heroes +80 (+300 when low), god +500, sticky +70, buildings never chosen (attack-move handles them); walkTo a quarter tile after every landed hit; retreat at 24% hp, back to full in spawn; tower safety: step 3 tiles away from a tower when it targets us below 65% hp or when no creep is beside us; portal home when 1-2 enemy heroes threaten the god and fewer than two defenders; buyback when respawn > 25 s and gold >= price + 200; gear dagger, armor, axe, portal, item 19, two heal potions; restock trip at 500 gold. It is the bundled base's push loop with a farmer's draft and discipline. A pusher out-scores our lane holder because the pusher's XP keeps coming from heroes and towers after the lane caps.

### Six-hour policy comparison (rounds 634-645, 288 replays, tools/gota_stats + gota_collate; stats_2026-09-22.md)
Per hero-game: khors (v56/v62 era) score 1,326, aaron 1,260, relh/richard 907, Jordan 724. What aaron does that we do not: 36% of its attack orders go at buildings (ours 4%), 4 building kills and 406 building XP a game (ours 0 and 40), 12% of alive time within 20 tiles of the enemy god (ours 8%), and it is on the winning team 74% of games (ours 54%). It keeps health higher (88% mean, 10% of time below half; ours 85% / 14%), dies 3 times to our 5, buys back 0 times to our 1 (231 vs 724 gold), and banks more unspent gold (1,310 vs 994). We out-farm and out-kill it (last-hit XP 1,892 vs 1,397; 10 kills vs 8) but lose the building income and the wins. relh/richard: 1,200 orders a minute, no attack-moves, poison, no potions, 3 buybacks a game. Jordan: a farmer (78% of orders at creeps, 97% alive, 4 kills).

Stats table rebuilt with accepted-only rates, spam and rejections (stats_2026-09-22.md). Our fighter issues 121 orders a minute, a third of them exact repeats of the previous order (40/min), and the engine rejects 42 a minute: 366 ActionOutOfRange and 92 ActionTargetUnavailable per game, nearly all ability casts aimed at a target beyond range (accepted casts are 1.5 a minute, not 35). aaron is the same shape (472 out-of-range per game). relh/richard: 1,409 orders and 1,134 repeats a minute, rejections for InventoryFull (268), NoCharges (121) and Cooldown (92). The idle bots spend the game issuing orders while dead (NotAlive 7,689 per game) or buying outside the keep (4,463).

### Backlog (user, 10:20 PDT)
- Building kills: prioritise barracks over towers. A dead barracks stops that lane's creep spawns for the enemy, which starves their farm and shared XP for the rest of the game; a tower only pays its 100 XP. Candidate: when a barracks (kind 5) is exposed within reach beside our creeps, score it above a tower and above plain creeps (barracks sit behind the towers, so "exposed" means the lane's towers are down). Measure building kills by kind in replays first (the stats extractor can split kind 4 and kind 5).

### ENGINE CHANGE at round 644 (2026-09-22 08:22 PDT): the league now runs coworld cow_2dd9158a (2026.9.22.2, replay version 56; polyworld commits 02897e8 "Fix GOTA symmetry and integer scores", 89ceec1, ab97f0b, ffcedcd "Balance heroes"). Guide: scores rounded down and clamped before averaging; all bots in a decision phase see the same frame (earlier bots' actions no longer change later observations); tile-boundary rounding symmetric by team; objects now ordered gods, buildings, heroes, creeps (allies first); simultaneous movement/attack resolution with seeded last-hit ties; hero balance changed (some class's HP per level halved, base damage up). Every arm since v90 (rounds 644+) ran on the OLD coworld; live rounds 645-647 with v92: 1,147 / 1,498 / 1,068. Aaron shipped "aaron-gota-balance0922-crossbow_draft" and "-coach" for the new build (round 647: 1,366 / 1,042). Re-baselining v92 on the new coworld; the extractor needs a version-56 build for rounds 644+.
Aaron's build-56 files (policy store): aaron-gota-balance0922-crossbow_draft = -coach = the previous micro0922 file plus "Crossbowman +300 in the draft"; four more variants (arcanist, crossbow, ranger, warlock, 612 lines) are uploaded but not competing. The balance commit (ffcedcd) halves one class's HP per level (38 to 19) and raises its base damage 46 to 69 and an ability's damage 58 to 87.

Why the XP tests over-rate us (15:50 PDT): our hero's score depends on whether aaron portals heroes are in the game. Field-d XP games: no aaron 2,040 (n 120), pair split 1,424 (24), pair on our team 899 (48), pair against us 787 (48). League rounds 653-657: no aaron 1,826 (11), one aaron 1,552 (42), pair split 653 (8), pair against us 211 (4); the aaron pair itself scores the same united or split (1,438 vs 1,433). Half of the field-d test games had no aaron at all, while 83% of our league games do, so the test mean (1,500) sits above the league reality (~1,200). Fix: gota_xp --league now takes field lines starred with * as ALWAYS PRESENT (they take the seats after ours and walk around the table with the slot, landing on both teams across bodies); field e = field d with aaron r3, aaron coach, richard v167, relh v161 and Jordan starred. New v114 baseline on field e (240) running; candidates from v135 on use it. Aaron's heroes take our XP whether friend or foe: they advance at the same god down the same lanes.

### ENGINE CHANGE (user notice, 2026-09-23 ~11:15 PDT): league coworld now cow_f2dcdbcd (2026.9.23.2, created 11:10 PDT, polyworld fd315c8 "Remove automatic spells, fix base casting"). The engine used to auto-cast for every BASIC hero (passive, then ultimate/secondary/primary at the current target, usually creeps); now abilities and items fire only on explicit castTarget/castPoint/useItem. khors already cast damage spells explicitly (heroes/buildings/god in reach, creeps only on a kill) and self-heals only while it had a target; it never cast Restore abilities (Arcanist Mana Crystal +28, Warlock Aether Siphon +30), which the engine used to fire for us. New host function abilityRestore(slot). Field unchanged (field j). v180 baseline on the new build: fk_v180_s0-9 (7cd6e0c6/75ce6b72/62a0f27e/c2fcbdba/f2ffaf56/67673a29/67c2e837/ce662e6c/13cb0c3c/632307c3), 240 games, pending.

### ENGINE CHANGE (user notice, 2026-09-23 ~14:30 PDT): league coworld now cow_9ff2e22a (2026.9.23.3, created 14:21 PDT, polyworld e42c482 "Add crowd control effects and status icons"). Four abilities trade ~20% damage for control: Vanguard R Blazing Blade stun 1 s (90 -> 72), Druid R Golem Seed root 2 s (85 -> 68), Lich E Bone Marionette root 1 s (66 -> 53), Warlock E Dread Totem silence 2 s (87 -> 70). Buildings and gods are immune. Stun stops everything; silence blocks abilities (new ActionSilenced); root blocks movement and teleports. New reads: selfSilenceTicks, objectStunTicks(i), objectSilenceTicks(i), objectRootTicks(i). Bundled base.bas: skips casts while silenced, ignores silenced casters as threats, and does not lead held targets.

AARON'S "FLAW IN THE GAME" (user request; players page metta-ai.github.io/polyworld-buff/GOTA/players/, 8-hour snapshot 12:11-20:11 UTC): poison potions (item 4, 40 gold, strike 35 at the current attack target within attack range, any object incl. heroes, towers, god) have NO cooldown: consumableCooldown() in sim.nim only gates heal, mana and portal items. A stack of 8 dumps 280 instant damage in one decision with last-hit credit. Aaron and coach buy ~20 poisons a game, BeWellBot 34, we 0. Aaron's file: ranged/caster heroes keep up to 8 poisons above a (deaths + 1) * 100 + 100 gold reserve and, when the attacked hero's hp <= 35 * stack (+ a basic hit about to land for Ranger/Crossbowman), throw exactly ceil(hp / 35) poisons in one tick; restock trip when fewer than 2 poisons. Other gaps in the snapshot: building attacks 27.5% vs our 8.7%, buildings destroyed 3.5 vs 1.4, deaths 4.6 vs 7.0; our recent average score (2,791) is already the highest in that window.

AARON'S REAL EDGE (user: poison is "not the major boost"): the SIEGE ANCHOR (R_siege_watch + R_siege_anchor in violet-tern). Engine asymmetry: a hero's reach to a building is measured to the nearest footprint point (sim.nim buildingAim), a tower's reach from its center (updateTower, TowerAttackRanges 300k/330k/360k by tier). A hero with range >= 270,000 standing at max range from a tower's edge can be outside the tower's reach. Aaron: when a hit lands on a tower whose objectTarget is 0, anchor there and keep attacking; abort (step 3 tiles away, 5 s cooldown) when the tower targets him or he loses hp; drop after 7 s without a hit. Fits his stats: 28% of attacks on buildings, 3.5 buildings a game, 4.6 deaths, Arcanist (300k range) first pick; tower kills pay 200 XP + 75 gold since 0924.

### ENGINE CHANGE (user notice, 2026-09-24 ~16:00 PDT): league coworld now cow_e282a46f (2026.9.24.2, created 15:47 PDT, polyworld f4456be "Fix tower range and homing attacks"). Tower range accounts for the footprint (no more outranging), reload continues when a tower loses its target, and fired shots home on their target beyond range and into fog. Tower vision/range 9 / 9.5 / 10 tiles. Also new: BASIC player mailboxes (d75e75b; not in the GotA guide yet). v189 baseline on the new build: fn_v189 (240, field l), with v192 rerun as fn_v192 (120).

FARMER (user go-ahead for a larger rewrite, 2026-09-27 ~21:00 PDT). Ari's farm loop: least-shared lane; farm point = a standoff behind the most advanced enemy creep of that lane (else the last farm point, where the next wave meets ours), capped at lane progress 72, pushed out of tower range; at the point last-hit creeps in range, hit a hero only when it is in basic range, never chase (drop the order when it leaves range). Ours: chases any hero within 30 tiles and pushes the enemy god after the lane waypoint.

### RULE CHANGE (user notice, 2026-09-28 ~17:00 PDT): EMMETT'S GLORY. League coworld cow_50c99dea (2026.9.28.1, polyworld 4507fc2 + 6951af1). Score = each WINNING hero's lifetime XP / elapsed simulated minutes (drafting included), rounded down; losses, draws and timeouts score 0; no per-minute penalty; the god kill still grants 1,000 XP to each winning hero. Farming without winning is now worth nothing; a fast win with decent XP scores highest (3,000 XP at 10.5 min = 285).

DETERMINISM DOES NOT CUT VARIANCE: any behaviour change diverges every game (2 of 230 paired games stay identical) and flips the outcome in ~17% of games each way, so paired games are as noisy as fresh ones. Power comes from volume: +3 points of win rate needs ~750 games per arm (~2 h per arm on the fast service).

Memberships are per league: khors:v278 stays competing/active champion in Gods of the Arena (round 1361: 19 of 19 games completed). Fix belongs on the platform side (move GOTA-RL to the current build or stop auto-enrolment); not ours to change.

**SEAT TEST (fast, 38 league rosters of rounds 1360-1361 x 5, only our seat swapped):** macromackie v9 in our seat W141 D26 L23 (74% wins, 0.811); Evolver v3 W141 D26 L23 (IDENTICAL games: Evolver is almost certainly the same bot as macromackie); James Botts v5 W93 D49 L48 (0.618); our v287 (Warlock seat) W72 D58 L60 (0.532). Paired vs v287: macromackie +0.279 t 8.34, James +0.087 t 2.49. Our five-Warlock team beats them head to head 60-0, but ONE macromackie seat lifts a mixed team far more than our one Warlock seat, and the ladder ranks by that. The current champion will not reach #1; the seat's mixed-team play must change. Next: v275 / v276 (macromackie-style Crossbowman builds) in our seat on the same rosters.

The gap is FINISHING: the macromackie seat does 61% of its team's damage to the god guards and god (4103 per game; ours 99); in the 10 pairs flipped toward it, guards down 1.8 vs 0.2 and ours draws at 20 min with ~5000 guard HP left. 3.0 of the 4.0 lost points come from seats 0 and 5 where our draft makes us the Crossbowman: it farms neutral camps (29 vs 6 kills), dies early to creeps, sits at home, and leaves the team 4 against 5. macromackie's seat pushes the short edge lane at 0:20-5:00 (first enemy outer tower at 2.7 min), mid at 6-11 min, the enemy base from 12 min (29% of alive time after 15 min vs our 4%), buys back 4.25 times, 137 last hits vs 77, 16.7 kills (mostly replacing teammates' kills). Our Warlock seat is close (W10 D4 L0 vs W13 L1).

WHY WE LOSE MIXED GAMES (user asked; from the seat diff): our win comes from teamwork that only exists when all five are khors (five attack-move mid together while four Warlocks heal through tower fire). In a mixed team our one seat is (a) a Warlock support with a weak attack that heals ~5200 a game but deals ~1% of its team's guard/god damage (macromackie's seat: 61%), so games without strong teammates time out with guards standing, or (b) a lone Crossbowman playing the five-stack plan alone (mid walk, jungle farming 29 vs 6, early creep deaths, team left 4 vs 5). macromackie's seat is self-sufficient: lane push with creeps, tower edge, 137 last hits, poison and potions, ~4 buybacks, and it tanks and kills the guards and god itself. Direction: in mixed teams our seat must be a self-sufficient finisher, not a support.
